## 2011 Germany National Championship

![](https://i.ormos.uk/73/29/7329cb2fe4242d77507434a2f994bacb515b8b228438c4a27d243bd918d04633.jpg)*Helge Holds a Full Nelson on Germany*

Have you ever played a game of **Magic**? Then you know that sometimes, you need to destroy an artifact or an enchantment, right? Well, tournament players all over the world seemed to have forgotten just that very lesson. Going into German Nationals, Standard appeared pretty much solved. [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)-[Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) was the overdog, U/B Control the hip anti-tech and [Valakut, the Molten Pinnacle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Valakut%252C%2Bthe%2BMolten%2BPinnacle) was the other deck.

Funny story: Turns out, sometimes you need something faster than [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated) to kill something. Like [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage), or [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension). That were two major angles of attack both [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)-[Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) and U/B Control had massive problems with, as champion Helge Nelson proved during the Standard rounds.

Nelson's story was remarkable. The 25-year-old PhD student originally was not qualified for Nationals and chose to try his luck and skill in the last-chance qualifiers on Thursday. When he entered his second *M12* draft tournament, skill and luck came together perfectly to carry him to a clean sweep 5-0 victory and the right to enter Nationals the next morning.

Nationals is a multi-format event and you cannot be the champion without also mastering the draft. *M12* proved to be an exciting, complex format that incited long discussions among the players how to make the best of it. Between the popular low-curve aggressive strategies, green-based aggro decks and blue-based control strategies, every player could find a niche.

When it came to the top 8 matches, [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)-[Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) was nowhere to be seen, beaten by U/B Control which in turn had difficulty dealing with [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt), [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) and especially [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage). The tables ran with fire as more than half of the Top 8 had red as their primary color. The German National Team – Helge Nelson, Tobias Dreger, Martin Zimmermann and Bernd Brendemühl – will certainly be looking at the red decks again come November and with it Worlds in San Francisco.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/8e/4e/8e4e5736e197a2d2790c94edb5ec1b7ef5028eb0e1ccf2b0a1c9ab7f9afdae20.jpg)  
*The German National Team (left to right): Martin Zimmermann, Tobias Dreger, Helge Nelson, and Bernd Brendemühl*

## Quarterfinal: Martin Zimmermann vs. Jan Hammerich

*by Tobi Henke* 

Martin Zimmermann entered the fray with blue-black control, Jan Hammerich brought his mono-red aggro deck. These were the two most succesful archetypes of the tournament, and here they faced off.

 

### Game One

Zimmermann won the die-roll, but started with two mulligans before playing a tapped land. Hammerich had [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide). "Now it's only one mulligan," Zimmermann commented, as he revealed [Darkslick Shores](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Darkslick+Shores) on the top of his library.

He had [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Inquisition%2Bof%2BKozilek) of [Kozilek, Butcher of Truth](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Kozilek%252C%2BButcher%2Bof%2BTruth), but didn't like what he saw in his opponent's hand: [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain)[Furnace Scamp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Furnace+Scamp), [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt), [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze) as well as two copies of [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage), one of which he took. Hammerich cast the other.

Zimmermann was looking for answers. Down came [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) and netted him an extra card. [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) attacked and Hammerich, stuck on two lands, summoned [Furnace Scamp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Furnace+Scamp) and [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer).

Zimmermann had [Black Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Black+Sun%27s+Zenith) to clear the board, but the clock of doom that is [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage) was still ticking. Unfortunately for Hammerich, he only had two lands and watched helplessly as his Shrine was returned to his hand via [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil). When he replayed it, it resolved uncontested, but now Zimmermann's deck was operating on full power. He cast [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Liliana+Vess) and, unbeknownst to his opponent, tutored up [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated). When Hammerich tapped low for [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler), [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Karn%2BLiberated) exiled the Shrine.

A [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) followed, which Hammerich killed with [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze) and [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt). Its little Zombie buddies started the beatdown anyway. Zimmermann soon found another [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) and Hammerich didn't have enough burn.

Martin Zimmermann 1 – 0 Jan Hammerich

Jan Hammerich didn't have a lot in his sideboard. He just switched two [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze)s for two copies of [Act of Aggression](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Act+of+Aggression). Meanwhile, Martin Zimmermann did some more extensive sideboarding:

\+ [Wurmcoil Engine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wurmcoil+Engine)  
 +3 [Disfigure](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disfigure)  
 +4 [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze)  
 +2 [Duress](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Duress)  
 +1 [Despise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Despise)  
 +1 [Black Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Black+Sun%27s+Zenith)  
 +1 [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember)

-3 [Solemn Simulacrum](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Solemn+Simulacrum)  
 -2 [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)  
 -1 [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated)  
 -1 [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge)  
 -1 [Consume the Meek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consume+the+Meek)  
 -1 [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx)  
 -4 [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren)

![](https://i.ormos.uk/ae/85/ae85941647da0f013092fe4611fb65be207bf0f2b0efb5c0d5060828e3fdb513.jpg)*Martin Zimmermann*

 

### Game Two

This time, Hammerich went to five. Zimmermann, after one mulligan, kept: [Darkslick Shores](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Darkslick+Shores), [Duress](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Duress), [Despise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Despise), [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek), and [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze).

Hammerich had a [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer), that attacked with bonus from [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks), while Zimmermann despised a [Hero of Oxid Ridge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hero+of+Oxid+Ridge). Next turn, Zimmermann used [Disfigure](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disfigure) on the [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Grim%2BLavamancer), but had still no second land. Hammerich had no play during his own turn, and didn't respond to Zimmermann's [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek). He revealed [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) and Hero of Oxid Rage. A baffled Zimmermann: "Err, yes? Then I take [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt)?" Hammerich chided himself for his misplay.

Zimmermann found lands, but Hammerich found [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage). For the rest of the game, he continued to draw spells, whereas, because of his earlier screw, Zimmermann's finishers came too late.

Martin Zimmermann 1 – 1 Jan Hammerich

 

### Game Three

[Despise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Despise) revealed: [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain), [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Koth+of+the+Hammer), [Furnace Scamp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Furnace+Scamp), [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt), [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze), [Scalding Tarn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Scalding+Tarn), and [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer). Zimmermann thought long and hard and, in the end, chose [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer). Hammerich summoned [Furnace Scamp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Furnace+Scamp).

Zimmermann preordained. When Hammerich decided to not sacrifice his [Furnace Scamp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Furnace+Scamp), Zimmermann killed it at end of turn. Hammerich had another [Furnace Scamp](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Furnace+Scamp), Zimmermann another [Disfigure](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disfigure).

[Chandra's Phoenix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chandra%27s+Phoenix), however, proved a more resilient threat. It attacked a couple of times, then was returned via [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil). It came back, then was killed by [Black Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Black+Sun%27s+Zenith). [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) regrew the Phoenix, but [Disfigure](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disfigure) killed it once again.

Zimmermann made a [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) that looked as if it might be fast enough to race the Phoenix. Zimmermann was at 10, Hammerich was at 18 from two fetchlands he had sacrificed earlier.

[Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) put Zimmermann at 7 and the Phoenix back in Hammerich's hand. The attack brought Hammerich to 5. [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) and his minions, together with [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit), took Hammerich to 5 as well. Hammerich had the [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) to clinch the game.

Martin Zimmermann 1 – 2 Jan Hammerich

![](https://i.ormos.uk/34/3b/343becfd8828ebb4dc0a6a555b4266c2038945c42c78c83666e1bf87101a6b1d.jpg)*Jan Hammerich*

 

### Game Four

Hammerich started with [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer), [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage), which met [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak), and another [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage), which resolved.

Zimmermann had [Black Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Black+Sun%27s+Zenith) for the [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Grim%2BLavamancer) and a [Disfigure](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disfigure) for a newly-summoned [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide). Hammerich wasn't able to make any creature stick, but he did have [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage) and was clearly planning on burning Zimmermann out, when he shot a [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) to the dome. Zimmermann on the other hand deployed [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan).

When [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) and team attacked for half of Hammerich's life total, Zimmermann himself was at 11, with [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage) on eight counters. At end of turn, Hammerich cast [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt); Zimmermann stopped it per [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze). The crowd held their breath. With three lands on the battlefield, an untapped land plus any one-mana spell would win the game for Hammerich. He topdecked ... [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks).

Martin Zimmermann 2 – 2 Jan Hammerich

 

### Game Five

Again, [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks) was responsible for less than optimal mana supply. Hammerich kept [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt), [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning), Hero of Oxid Rige, [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer), two [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide)s, and [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks) as the only land. Zimmermann agreed that it was the correct decision to keep this hand, but the resulting slow offense allowed Zimmermann to take full advantage of all his discard.

Soon, Hammerich was left with nothing but a lone [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) on the battlefield, along with three lands. He only had a [Hero of Oxid Ridge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hero+of+Oxid+Ridge) and an [Act of Aggression](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Act+of+Aggression) in hand, when Zimmermann reached six mana. But the control player knew about the [Act of Aggression](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Act+of+Aggression) and played [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) first, and only then continued with [Wurmcoil Engine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wurmcoil+Engine). The fatties finished the game in short order.

Martin Zimmermann 3 – 2 Jan Hammerich

## Quarterfinal Roundup

*by Hanno Terbuyken* 

While the quintessential match-up of this Nationals got the in-depth treatment (Mono-Red vs. U/B Control), I had the other three to watch.

 

### Michael Müller (U/B Control) vs. Tobias Dreger ([Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension))

Starting with Müller vs. Dreger, Gixatian [Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Probe) showed Dreger Müller's mighty hand of [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak), [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil), [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Liliana+Vess), Jace Blereren, [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge), [Despise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Despise) and [Stoic Rebuttal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stoic+Rebuttal). Müller drew an [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island) off the top and Dreger had to spend a [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) to stop Müller's [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren). Dreger had [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) on the battlefield, but things were looking good for Müller.

A couple of minutes later, Dreger had two [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) with two counters and as I walked over, he pointed [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) and Red Sun's Zenith at Brendemühl, who shrugged and picked up his cards.

 

### Helge Nelson (Mono-Red) vs. Michael Diezel (Mono-Red)

Meanwhile, Nelson and Diezel had already finished game one in their match. Both players had slung burn and creatures at each other, but Diezel had a not-so-subtle surprise for Nelson: Two [Goblin Grenade](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Grenade)s. Boom.

 

### Bernd Brendemühl (Mono-Red) vs. Fabian Görzgen ([Valakut, the Molten Pinnacle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Valakut%252C%2Bthe%2BMolten%2BPinnacle))

![](https://i.ormos.uk/73/a7/73a7ca695bd4d00a528e97521f3a41c1b8c9aad311b471313b659957d8c38432.jpg)*Bernd Brendemühl*

On the table to their right, Görzgen threw the first game away on a technicality. With exactly seven mana available including [Overgrown Battlement](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Overgrown+Battlement)s, he moved to cast [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan) and realized that he had not intended to use the [Overgrown Battlement](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Overgrown+Battlement). Since he had announced the spell already, he had to play it that way. With the Titan on the stack, Görzgen was tapped out and thus without any chance of [Nature's Claim](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Nature%27s+Claim) to gain critical life. Brendemühl could incinerate Görzgen for the last three points of damage. Görzgen did not have [Nature's Claim](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Nature%27s+Claim) in hand, but that didn't matter in the least, because his misplay removed any uncertainty on Brendemühl's part that he could win game one right there.

 

### Helge Nelson (Mono-Red) vs. Michael Diezel (Mono-Red)

![](https://i.ormos.uk/96/88/9688f741855c2506dd884fb1b6bebe6682cb40461701e65606b9676f90b6a2bf.jpg)*Helge Nelson*

While Brendemühl and Görzgen shored up game one, the two accomplished burn mages Nelson and Diezel had already finished game two, this one going to Nelson as Diezel had missed a land to enable landfall on [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze), playing the entire game on two lands. Nelson fared better to pull away with damage and the win.

 

### Michael Müller (U/B Control) vs. Tobias Dreger ([Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension))

Over in the match where a lot was going on without a lot happening, Müller held a good grip of [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek), [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze), [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain), [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated) and [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren). However, Dreger kept harassing him with [Mental Misstep](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mental+Misstep) and [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe), proving that the information advantage was as important as the extra cards he drew off [Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Probe)s. [Foresee](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Foresee) showed Dreger that he had land, [See Beyond](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=See+Beyond), [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) and [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder) on top of his library. "Those all stay on top", he announced, cleary pleased with that selection.

Müller played [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren). Dreger baited [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) with [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) and followed it up immediately with a second one that Müller couldn't stop, as laughter from the mono-red match drew my attention away: Helge Nelson had just pulled ahead a game in this best-of-five match.

The match between Müller and Dreger not only was entertaining to watch for the player's easygoing banter, but also for the game itself. Where Müller had added [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Liliana+Vess) to his board, Dreger again had Pyromancer Ascension going with two counters. A replacement [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren), [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated) and Wurmcoil Enginge were still waiting in Müller's hand. But Dreger [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder)ed without end. [Red Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Red+Sun%27s+Zenith) was in sight on top of his library, [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) was in his hand and he held two [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)s to protect all that.

Müller used [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Liliana%2BVess) to find what he needed. But Dreger's deck was running smoothly, and he shaped his hand to deal with whatever Müller could throw at him, like [Volition Reins](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Volition+Reins). Müller was still on 20 life so Dreger would need the full force of [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) to deal enough damage.

 

### Helge Nelson (Mono-Red) vs. Michael Diezel (Mono-Red)

![](https://i.ormos.uk/d3/0c/d30cfff0b072d5d32b199a5795172ea172c12cf1371ca7bdec1d66af550c336d.jpg)*Michael Diezel*

Enough damage was not a problem for Helge Nelson, though, who had secured a third win in his match and was the first player to advance to the semifinals of the German Nationals 2011! In the fourth game of the mono-red mirror, he had managed to equip [Sword of War and Peace](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+War+and+Peace), "which should never happen", according to Diezel. [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) had come from the top of his library to pick up the beatstick, and Diezel never recovered from that.

 

### Michael Müller (U/B Control) vs. Tobias Dreger ([Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension))

Back to the more interesting match, Dreger had almost come in danger of drawing his entire library. He started the shooting with [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) and [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) to get Müller to 13 life and kill [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Liliana+Vess), as Müller was unable to cast either one of his two [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze)s to stop any of that. Dreger was on a mere 8 life, though, and Müller contemplated if his two [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit)s could kill Dreger safely without dying from a burn spell.

 

### Bernd Brendemühl (Mono-Red) vs. Fabian Görzgen ([Valakut, the Molten Pinnacle](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Valakut%252C%2Bthe%2BMolten%2BPinnacle))

![](https://i.ormos.uk/1c/f4/1cf4b0041b1093eb19e23ddf6294e0e8e8bd9971a3f6cd8d197762221c888230.jpg)*Fabian Görzgen*

While all of this was happening, Brendemühl and Görzgen were plodding away in their quarterfinal, which turned out to be a one-sided affair. In game two of the match, Brendemühl had kicked off with [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) and [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks), dealing a hefty amount of damage before Görzgen could establish his wall defense. But Görzgen found nothing else to stop the attacks – Primeval Titan especially eluded him. When Görzgen was on 8 life, Brendemühl drew [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler), [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) and [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate), in that order, and fried Görzgen thorougly.

Game three was essentially a blowout after both players mulliganed. Görzgen played a turn five [Avenger of Zendikar](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Avenger+of+Zendikar), but couldn't grow his plants for lack of mana. Brendemühl seized the opportunity to kill the Avenger and found [Kargan Dragonlord](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kargan+Dragonlord). It took just one turn to get the Dragonlord to eight counters, and Brendemühl rode that to victory and into the semifinals!

 

### Michael Müller (U/B Control) vs. Tobias Dreger ([Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension))

Back in game two of the combo-control duel, Dreger had not managed to get an Ascension back up yet. At 3 life, he was in severe danger of dying to [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit) next turn, but with an end-of-turn [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning), Dreger took Brendemühl from 7 to 5, giving his [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) a second counter. Dreger untapped, attacked with his [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) (Müller to 1 life) and took a close game two with a final burn spell.

Game three was once more a prolonged affair. The cheerfulness had largely dried out from these players, as they were playing mentally exhausting **Magic**, each waiting for a [Mental Misstep](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mental+Misstep) from the other. Müller picked up a warning as he failed to discard down to 7 cards at the end of one of his turns. Roughly fifteen minutes into the game the board position consisted of lands only and Dreger had just two cards left in his library. Müller had sucessfully prevented any and all [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) shenanigans, and this time, Dreger had not found a chink in the blue-black armor.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/da/5d/da5d97978502c3b50cad2d8561b787682566cd8291d4d2653f10487533ff833b.jpg)*Tobias Dreger (left) and Michael Müller (right)*

At two games to Dreger and one to Müller, the players shuffled up for the fourth game. This time, Dreger fared much better, setting up with [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) with two counters AND a [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) about ten turns into the game. Müller had [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Liliana+Vess) to help him out, though the planeswalker did little to afffect the board.

[Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe) showed Dreger [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze), [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated), [Surgical Extraction](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Surgical+Extraction), [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx), Jace Beleren and [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) in Müller's hand, but Müller was tapped out anyway and could do nothing as Dreger pushed a second [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) to two counters. "Drawing cards is good", sighed Müller as his opponent drew and drew and drew through his deck. That Müller was stuck on five lands barely helped. Two times he tried [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek), but with the Sphinx providing twice the cards Müller had, Dreger had a counter for each of them.

Müller then tried [Surgical Extraction](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Surgical+Extraction), on [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak). Dreger responded with [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) with Kicker, copied twice – enough to kill Müller on the spot. "No, you cannot see my hand!" said Dreger and took the fourth game and the match.

### Final Results

**Bernd Brendemühl 3 – 0 Fabian Görzgen**

**Helge Nelson 3 – 1 Michael Diezel**

**Michael Müller 1 – 3 Tobias Dreger**

## Third-place playoff - Bernd Brendemühl vs. Martin Zimmermann

*by Hanno Terbuyken* 

Both Brendemühl and Zimmermann came to the table quite relaxed. They knew that they wouldn't have to face the pressure of a championship match. Both had qualified for Worlds, and nobody would take that away from them now.

 

### **Game One**

 

Brendemühl started with the classic opening move of [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain), [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide), attacked and gave Zimmermann two extra lands in the first two turns of the game. That was good enough for Zimmermann, who was happy to then kill the Goblin with [Go for the Throat](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Go+for+the+Throat). Brendemühl offered [Plated Geopede](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plated+Geopede) buffed by [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks), for which Zimmermann had Doomblade.

[Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) let Zimmermann remove [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate) from Brendemühl's hand from among Burst Lightning, [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler) and [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer). His Jace was quickly attacked and killed, but Zimmermann had already drawn [Consume the Meek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consume+the+Meek) to clear the board of enemy creatures. Brendemühl tried to rebuild with [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer), which promptly died to another Go for the Throat. Whatever Brendemühl did, Zimmermann had the answer.

Next answer: [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan). Done.

Bernd Brendemühl 0 – 1 Martin Zimmermann

![](https://i.ormos.uk/fc/0e/fc0e8cb5e0dcaed9d50d19610995d03f09490d4830ae9dd800728273dbde7f68.jpg)*Bernd Brendemühl*

"Tell me, are you leaving the [Kargan Dragonlord](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kargan+Dragonlord)s in?" Zimmmermann asked jokingly. Brendemühl politely declined to reveal his sideboard plan to his opponent. He did reveal it to the coverage reporter, though. Brendemühl brought in 1 [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Koth+of+the+Hammer) and his 3 [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage), taking out 3 [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze) and 1 [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning)

Zimmermann's sideboard plan was more complex. He swapped 12 cards from his deck:

\- 3 [Solemn Simulacrum](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Solemn+Simulacrum)  
 - 1 [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated)  
 - 1 [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge)  
 - 1 [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx)  
 - 2 [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak)  
 - 4 [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren)

\+ 1 [Despise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Despise)  
 + 3 [Disfigure](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disfigure)  
 + 1 [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember)  
 + 2 [Duress](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Duress)  
 + 4 [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze)  
 + 1 [Wurmcoil Engine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wurmcoil+Engine)

 

### **Game Two**

 

An early [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) answered Zimmermann's question about the sideboard plan immediately: In Brendemühl's initial seven, he found and discarded [Kargan Dragonlord](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kargan+Dragonlord). Still, Brendemühl had enough gas to put pressure on Zimmermann. [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) did a lot of the work, dealing 10 damage on its own, taking Zimmermann to 7 life with the help of [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate).

Brendemühl played Shrine of [Searing Flesh](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Flesh) and [Plated Geopede](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plated+Geopede). With just two lands in play, drawing a third only on his eighth turn, Brendemühl's deck couldn't operate on full power. Still, his cards were cheap and he pointed more burn at Zimmermann as soon as he got it. [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) and [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) brought Zimmermann to 2 life. All Brendemühl needed to finish his opponent was some burn spell.

But Zimmmermann had brought [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) to the battlefield once more, and Brendemühl's time to win was running out. [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) wasn't doing much against the Titan, and when Brendemühl's last chance turned out to be [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Koth+of+the+Hammer), Zimmermann had turned the game around at the last moment.

"This was close", commented Zimmermann, "any closer would have been death!"

Bernd Brendemühl 0 – 2 Martin Zimmermann

 

### **Game Three**

 

Once more, Zimmermann led with his favorite card, [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek). He saw Kargan Dragonlord, [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt), [Hero of Oxid Ridge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hero+of+Oxid+Ridge), [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate), [Plated Geopede](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plated+Geopede) and [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain), picking the Dragonlord. [Go for the Throat](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Go+for+the+Throat) killed the [Plated Geopede](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plated+Geopede) that was Brendemühl's first attack, and Zimmermann had to eat a lot of damage when he played another [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Inquisition%2Bof%2BKozilek), because Brendemühl dealt him six in response to the spell with [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) and [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate). He also got to keep his other two cards, because the [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Inquisition%2Bof%2BKozilek) could not touch the two [Hero of Oxid Ridge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hero+of+Oxid+Ridge).

Again, Brendemühl was scarce on lands, not drawing a fourth for three turns. [Kargan Dragonlord](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kargan+Dragonlord) filled the gap in the meanwhile. Despite being able to cast his [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember)s, Zimmermann was also hit by mana troubles, as he failed to find a source for blue mana. Brendemühl declined to give him a chance for land through [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide), and when blue mana was not forthcoming, Zimmermann succumbed to the onslaught.

Bernd Brendemühl 1 – 2 Martin Zimmermann

![](https://i.ormos.uk/0d/e2/0de2471d17a2fca4c64af11d64aa9b85febaf7d542ce0a6036fef0e320e3d12c.jpg)*Martin Zimmermann and his Playoff winning neckline.*

 

### **Game Four**

 

Immediately after he drew his initial seven cards in the fourth game, Zimmermann shipped them back for a better six. [Despise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Despise) then gave him a look at four lands, [Kargan Dragonlord](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kargan+Dragonlord), [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer) and [Plated Geopede](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plated+Geopede). Brendemühl drew [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) on his next turn, played it and gave Zimmermann a land with it. The next card he revealed was [Consume the Meek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consume+the+Meek) – potentially bad news for Brendemühl.

Brendemühl smashed Zimmermann to 14 with [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) and played [Kargan Dragonlord](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kargan+Dragonlord), attacking with it and [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) on the next turn. Zimmermann decided to take 4 damage, going to 10, and only after combat did Brendemühl put counters on the Dragonlord. [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate) brought Zimmermann to 7. [Consume the Meek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consume+the+Meek) prevented more damage from the coming attack. Brendemühl had [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) to put Zimmermann on 4, and [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler) sacrificed brought Zimmermann to 2.

With Doomblades, [Disfigure](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disfigure)s and [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze)s, Zimmermann kept the board empty for while. He fought off five [Plated Geopede](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plated+Geopede)s, [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler)s and [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide)s. Then Brendemühl drew another [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) from the top, and the little Goblin that did so much this weekend finished the job to even out the games.

Bernd Brendemühl 2 – 2 Martin Zimmermann

 

### **Game Five**

 

So it came down to a fifth game! Both players tackled it in relaxed fashion. Both would be going to Worlds, and that was the main goal. A place on the National team was just the icing on the cake. But still: Who would it be?

Brendemühl kicked off with [Plated Geopede](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plated+Geopede), Zimmermann answered with [Disfigure](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disfigure). The second [Plated Geopede](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plated+Geopede) met [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze). [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) took away an [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler), but another one remained alongside two [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) and [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate).

Zimmermann went to 17 from [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) and used [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) to stop [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage). The next creature Brendemühl played was [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler), then [Kargan Dragonlord](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kargan+Dragonlord). [Duress](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Duress) from Zimmermann just revealed more of the same: [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt), [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate), [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning). [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate) went away, [Disfigure](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Disfigure) took care of [Kargan Dragonlord](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kargan+Dragonlord) and finally Zimmermann knew what he wanted to use all the time for that he bought: [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan).

He didn't have much joy of it, though. Brendemühl burned the Titan away with [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt), [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) and a sacrificed [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler). That emptied Brendemühl's hand completely, and Ziommermann's two zombie tokens were the only non-land permanents left. Facing a control deck in full stride with nothing left to play with but the top of his library, Brendemühl had no outs.

**Bernd Brendemühl 2 – 3 Martin Zimmermann**

## Semifinal - Tobias Dreger vs. Martin Zimmermann

*by Hanno Terbuyken* 

 

### Game One

Zimmermann kicked off the information war with [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek), taking away [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe) from Dreger while also seeing [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak), [Red Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Red+Sun%27s+Zenith) and [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt). [Clear](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Clear)ly Zimmermann didn't want his hand given away. But Dreger had another [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe), and [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) for Zimmermann's second [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Inquisition%2Bof%2BKozilek). Zimmermann was happy to see no [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) in Dregers hand, but two turns later, Dreger simply drew one and put it on the battlefield to the crowd's amusement.

As in his quarterfinal match, Dreger burned through cards like only the Ascension deck can. That gave him [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) to counter Zimmermann's [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) – an important spell, as that one gave Dreger the second counter on his [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension). Dreger then played a second Ascension and [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder)ed and Foresaw and Saw Beyond to quickly put a second counter on that one, too. A bunch of draw and search spells later, Zimmermann conceded game one.

Tobias Dreger 1 – 0 Martin Zimmermann

![](https://i.ormos.uk/a8/43/a84300468be5259eda7c6d494d03e613af7cc386b33a6b5006215076550cbb48.jpg)*Tobias Dreger*

Both players went to their sideboards and changed their decks. Tobias Dreger simply took out 4 [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) and added 3 [Mental Misstep](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mental+Misstep) and 1 [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) from his sideboard.

Martin Zimmermann's plan was a little more intricate. His changes looked like this:

### IN

\+ 4 [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze)  
 + 2 [Memoricide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Memoricide)  
 + 2 [Duress](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Duress)

### OUT

\- 1 [Solemn Simulacrum](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Solemn+Simulacrum)  
 - 1 [Black Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Black+Sun%27s+Zenith)  
 - 1 [Go for the Throat](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Go+for+the+Throat)  
 - 2 [Doom Blade](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Doom+Blade)  
 - 1 [Consume the Meek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consume+the+Meek)  
 - 1 [Despise](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Despise)  
 - 1 [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember)

 

### Game Two

Once more, Zimmermann decided to put an [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) out as a first feeler. He saw 2 [See Beyond](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=See+Beyond), [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder), [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain), [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) and 2 fetchlands in Dreger's opening hand and made him discard the [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder). Dreger had drawn a [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe), though, and returned the favor of probing for information. He saw 2 [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren), [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit) and two more lands.

The two players essentially replayed their first couple of turns without much difference – once again, Zimmermann took a [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder) from Dreger's hand. But Dreger simply saw more cards from his deck overall, and one of them was [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) that immediately received two counters through the usual small spells.

Zimmermann's main problem was that his deck had no way to deal with [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) on the battlefield other than one [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated) and one [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil). Though Zimmermann played [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Liliana+Vess) to help him find a solution, his biggest hope were his counterspells. But even those would never be enough to stop all copies.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/cc/83/cc834608ea3d04d2bbe1dd339f160810b8f904d640c00573d0f606ef98dcb5c2.jpg)*'...When he put a third Pyromancer Ascension on the battlefield and pumped it up to two counters, Zimmermann slumped...'*

A case in point was the [Red Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Red+Sun%27s+Zenith) that Dreger played to kill [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Liliana+Vess). Zimmermann tried [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze) to counter at least one copy, to keep [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Liliana%2BVess) alive. But Dreger not only had a copy of Zenith, but also [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) to stop the [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze) entirely. And of course, Dreger simply drew more cards. When he put a third [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) on the battlefield and pumped it up to two counters, Zimmermann slumped.

However, Dreger was already down to 8 life, mostly damaging himself through fetchlands. Zimmermann attacked with [Creeping Tar Pit](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Creeping+Tar+Pit). But against three loaded Ascensions, that plan seemed much too slow – and it was, as it turned out, especially since Dreger still had [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) to just kill it.

Tobias Dreger 2 – 0 Martin Zimmermann

 

### Game Three

Zimmermann decided to play first and immediately mulliganed to six cards in hand. Neither player had [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) or [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe), so for the first few turns the fog of war engulfed their hands. Until Dreger had [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe). Zimmermann sat on a hand of [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze), [Solemn Simulacrum](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Solemn+Simulacrum), [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren), [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) and [Go for the Throat](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Go+for+the+Throat) – but he was missing land drops.

Four mana was just enough to play [Memoricide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Memoricide), though. Dreger had to let it resolve, with a hand of [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt), [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning), [See Beyond](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=See+Beyond), [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder) – and [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension). Of course, that was what Zimmermann had in mind to name anyway! Without his Ascensions, Dreger had to try and win this game the hard way, with Bolts, Bursts, [Red Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Red+Sun%27s+Zenith) and [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx).

A reinvigorated Zimmermann was happy to summon [Solemn Simulacrum](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Solemn+Simulacrum) to the battlefield and to let Dreger dig through his deck with [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder) and [See Beyond](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=See+Beyond). [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) followed from Zimmermann, but did nothing: [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) from Dreger drew out [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) from Zimmermann, but after an untap a second Bolt killed Jace for good and Dreger used [Red Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Red+Sun%27s+Zenith) to remove [Solemn Simulacrum](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Solemn+Simulacrum).

Zimmermann was unfazed. Small wonder, as he tapped all his lands to put [Grave Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grave+Titan) on the battlefield! Dreger was holding [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx) which he played, knowing full well that Zimmermann was still holding [Go for the Throat](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Go+for+the+Throat), but the feeble resistance was futile and Martin took the game.

Tobias Dreger 2 – 1 Martin Zimmermann

![](https://i.ormos.uk/1c/00/1c008fe4c39a1b018cdac2ac5d8c911e9813a0fbca89dad6d602534c5fbb67f1.jpg)*Tobias Dreger's double ascensions overwhelm Zimmerman's U/B control.*

 

### Game Four

Dreger pondered long over his initial hand and decided to ship it back for lack of land. [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inquisition+of+Kozilek) from Zimmermann revealed that his second set had enough land, but only [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe) and [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) as spells, and the [Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Probe) quickly went away. A turn later, Zimmermann played a second [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Inquisition%2Bof%2BKozilek), which Dreger countered with his [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak). A third [Inquisition of Kozilek](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Inquisition%2Bof%2BKozilek) was met by [Mental Misstep](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mental+Misstep). With that, Dreger had successfully protected the [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) he had just drawn to put it on the battlefield.

[Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe) showed Dreger that Zimmermann held [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren), [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze), [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Liliana+Vess), [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) and [Consecrated Sphinx](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Consecrated+Sphinx). But all of that wasn't much help as Dreger exploded in a flurry of copied cantrips.

A second [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) immediately gained two counters as well. Zimmermann slouched in his chair after he had pressed F6 to cede priority to Dreger for the turn. There was nothing he could do, and after Dreger countered [Liliana Vess](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Liliana+Vess), Martin extended the hand and Tobias Dreger advanced to the finals!

**Tobias Dreger 3 – 1 Martin Zimmermann**

## Semifinal - Bernd Brendemühl vs. Helge Nelson

*by Tobi Henke* 

"Burning" Bernd Brendemühl is known throughout Germany as one of the very best mono red mages. Sitting across from him this round, however, was Helge Nelson who had already dispatched Michael Diezel in another mono red mirror match in the quarterfinals.

 

### Game One

"My start's rather harmless, I'm afraid," said Brendemühl, playing [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks) and passing the turn. Nelson had [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) and attacked. On his turn, Brendemühl got rid of the Goblin via [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze). Next, his [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) killed [Plated Geopede](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plated+Geopede). Then, Nelson summoned [Chandra's Phoenix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chandra%27s+Phoenix) which received a [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) as well.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/69/a7/69a7aaeb4cf527c95defd85cdae3661cf98bed1448652435bfcccc6172799379.jpg)*Bernd Brendemühl*

Brendemühl made the first creature of his own: [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler).
