Nelson had yet more creatures: Another [Plated Geopede](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plated+Geopede), and two [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer)s. Brendemühl killed all of them, losing his [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler) in the process. And it was just as well because Nelson had cast [Sword of War and Peace](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+War+and+Peace). With the equipment at the ready and Brendemühl tapped out, Nelson looked at the top of his deck. "[Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide)?"

He got [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Koth+of+the+Hammer) instead and attacked for 4. Brendemühl who was stuck on three lands made [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer) and [Kargan Dragonlord](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kargan+Dragonlord) and passed the turn with two cards left in his hand, resigning to the fate of being hit by [Sword of War and Peace](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sword+of+War+and+Peace). Nelson drew [Chandra's Phoenix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chandra%27s+Phoenix), cast and equipped it, with the fifth mana courtesy of [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Koth%2Bof%2Bthe%2BHammer), then attacked for, all in all, 6 damage to put Brendemühl at 6.

"I don't even have the mana to get rid of enough cards in my hand to survive one more turn," Brendemühl complained.

Bernd Brendemühl 0 – 1 Helge Nelson

### Nelson's sideboarding

-4 [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide)  
 -2 [Hero of Oxid Ridge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hero+of+Oxid+Ridge)  
 +2 [Manic Vandal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Manic+Vandal)  
 +2 [Arc Trail](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Arc+Trail)  
 +1 [Vulshok Refugee](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vulshok+Refugee)  
 +1 [Dismember](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dismember)

### Brendemühl's sideboarding

-4 [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide)  
 -4 [Kargan Dragonlord](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Kargan+Dragonlord)  
 -1 [Hero of Oxid Ridge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hero+of+Oxid+Ridge)  
 +3 [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage)  
 +2 [Vulshok Refugee](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vulshok+Refugee)  
 +2 [Manic Vandal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Manic+Vandal)  
 +1 [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze)  
 +1 [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Koth+of+the+Hammer)

 

### Game Two

Both decks apparently turned a lot more control-oriented after sideboarding. The action started with [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage) for Nelson, [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler) for Brendemühl. Nelson had another Shrine, then hit [Ember Hauler](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ember+Hauler) with [Arc Trail](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Arc+Trail).

Over the next couple of turns, the players traded numerous burn spells for just as many creatures. The board was empty except for lands on both sides and the two Shrines on Nelson's side. He controlled one Shrine with seven counters and one with eight as well as six lands. Brendemühl was on 17, Nelson himself on 10. Nelson drew the only card that would allow him to win this turn: [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Koth+of+the+Hammer). If he played [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Koth%2Bof%2Bthe%2BHammer), the Shrines would both get another counter. Then he could use [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Koth%2Bof%2Bthe%2BHammer)'s second ability to generate six mana, and deal lethal damage with the Shrines. But Nelson didn't see it!

![](https://i.ormos.uk/4e/7a/4e7a4e2dd5ae4850f617f7005491053e47ffff0c05b6cb9a7a3677fa8d9e9aee.jpg)*Helge Nelson produces an Arc Trail to deal with Brendemühl's Ember Hauler*

He used [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Koth%2Bof%2Bthe%2BHammer)'s first ability instead and attacked for 4. Brendemühl pointed out the mistake and also that he himself did indeed have 7 points of burn damage (kicked [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning), which he played at end of turn, and [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt)) in his hand. If he drew another [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) or [Incinerate](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Incinerate) now, Nelson's mistake really would cost him the game. The crowd watched expectantly as Brendemühl slowly drew his card: It was [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Koth+of+the+Hammer), now completely useless because of Nelson's own [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Koth%2Bof%2Bthe%2BHammer).

Bernd Brendemühl 0 – 2 Helge Nelson

 

### Game Three

After mulligans, Brendemühl was a little short on mana, which delayed his [Vulshok Refugee](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vulshok+Refugee). Meanwhile his opponent's [Chandra's Phoenix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chandra%27s+Phoenix) was busy attacking since turn three.

Still, the Refugee wasn't exactly bad at playing catch up. After all, it did have more power, and [Chandra's Phoenix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chandra%27s+Phoenix) died a couple of times. Just in time, though, Nelson found [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage) to get rid of the Refugee.

Nevertheless, [Vulshok Refugee](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vulshok+Refugee) and [Chandra's Phoenix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chandra%27s+Phoenix) had managed to put their respective victims in the single-digit life total range. The game was going to be decided on direct damage. In the end, it came down to a situation when Nelson's [Staggershock](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Staggershock) rebound put Brendemühl at exactly 1 life, so he couldn't use [Arid Mesa](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Arid+Mesa) to get landfall for his [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze).

**Bernd Brendemühl 0 – 3 Helge Nelson**

## Final: Tobias Dreger vs. Helge Nelson

*by Tobi Henke* 

This is it. The culmination of one weekend of thrilling **Magic**. Three more wins and one of these players would be crowned champion of German Nationals 2011. The match-up was [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) played by Tobias Dreger and, on the other side, a deck and a player not many had expected at the beginning of the weekend to end up here. Helge Nelson won a last-chance qualifier on Thursday and made it all the way to the finals with his mono-red deck.

 

### **Game One**

 

Dreger won the die-roll and kept his seven, Nelson took two mulligans. Both players had tapped lands on their first turn, [Halimar Depths](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Halimar+Depths) for Dreger, [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks) for Nelson.

On turn two, Nelson summoned two [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide)s, one of which was stopped by [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak). Dreger cast [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) and passed the turn with one [Mountain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mountain) untapped. Nelson used the opportunity and cast [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage) as well as a third [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide). One of the Guides died to [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning), the other got Dreger down to 16.

Dreger cast [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain), paid 2 life for [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe), saw that the single card in Nelson's hand was [Staggershock](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Staggershock), paid 1 more life to fetch [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island) with [Misty Rainforest](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Misty+Rainforest), and cast another [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) to put the first counter on [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension). He passed the turn with no blue mana available, an opportunity Nelson used to resolve [Staggershock](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Staggershock).

[Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) was hit by [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt). Now, since Nelson was tapped out and couldn't use his Shrine, Dreger seized the opportunity and bounced the artifact. He passed the turn with [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) mana up. Nelson didn't have much choice and re-cast his Shrine and Dreger did indeed have the [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak), which also put a second counter on [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension)

Now on 9 life with a fully-powered Ascension, Dreger looked firmly in control. The game continued for a couple of turns in a flurry of copied [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain)s, [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder)s, [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe)s, with the occasional [See Beyond](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=See+Beyond) for good measure. Nelson fought back valiantly with [Chandra's Phoenix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chandra%27s+Phoenix), a second [Staggershock](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Staggershock), and [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt). And in fact, he did manage to get Dreger down to 4. But when a second [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) made its way to the table and was charged in record time, a couple of Lightnings soon finished the game

Tobias Dreger 1 – 0 Helge Nelson

The match-up was about to change considerably, because both players boarded some extremely effective cards, though it was hard to say who gained most from his sideboard.

Nelson's sideboarding:

-4 [Searing Blaze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Searing+Blaze)  
 -1 [Staggershock](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Staggershock)  
 +2 [Manabarbs](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Manabarbs)  
 +2 [Manic Vandal](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Manic+Vandal)  
 +1 [Vulshok Refugee](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vulshok+Refugee)

Dreger's sideboarding:  
 -4 [Gitaxian Probe](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gitaxian+Probe)  
 -4 [See Beyond](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=See+Beyond)  
 -1 [Foresee](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Foresee)  
 -1 [Red Sun's Zenith](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Red+Sun%27s+Zenith)  
 -2 [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil)  
 +2 [Batterskull](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Batterskull)  
 +4 [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze)  
 +3 [Pyroclasm](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyroclasm)  
 +3 [Mental Misstep](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mental+Misstep)

![](https://i.ormos.uk/1b/39/1b39e2df3cff020005036d2842fb86c25f82a9ff1fe0668ef615ea919f5aa42a.jpg)*Tobias Dreger*

 

### **Game Two**

 

Both players kept their opening seven, neither had a play on turn one. Turn two, Nelson had [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer), whereas Dreger had [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension). What Dreger lacked, however, was blue mana. Nelson attacked, summoned [Plated Geopede](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Plated+Geopede), and played a [Scalding Tarn](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Scalding+Tarn).

Dreger still didn't have an [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island), but he did have a [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt) for the Geopede. Dreger was in dire trouble as Nelson cast [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Koth+of+the+Hammer), which Dreger couldn't stop without blue mana. Dreger killed it with two [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning)s which got him a first counter on [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension), but not without taking 5 damage on the attack.

Again no [Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Island), while Nelson made [Hero of Oxid Ridge](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Hero+of+Oxid+Ridge), exactly when Dreger was out of instant burn. He did have [Pyroclasm](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyroclasm) to take out Hero and [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer), but Nelson simply untapped, and pointed two [Lightning Bolt](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lightning+Bolt)s and a [Staggershock](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Staggershock) at Dreger's face

Tobias Dreger 1 – 1 Helge Nelson

 

### **Game Three**

 

Dreger's start was much better this time, with [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain), [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension), [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain), [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder), and [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning), all in the first three turns.

Nelson's draw was certainly not bad either, with [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer), [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage), and [Vulshok Refugee](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vulshok+Refugee). Nelson continued with [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer) and left three mana up for [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage).

Dreger had [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) for the [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Grim%2BLavamancer), putting a second counter on his Ascension. But on his turn, when this reporter was expecting a number of spells and copies, Dreger just laid a land and passed. A quick peek at his hand confirmed: he only held red cards, and a [Batterskull](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Batterskull) which was still one turn away from being cast.

[Staggershock](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Staggershock), its rebound, [Vulshok Refugee](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vulshok+Refugee), and [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage) ended the game before it ever came to that.

Tobias Dreger 1 – 2 Helge Nelson

![](https://i.ormos.uk/a0/42/a0425dcdbae4160c140b5ef41eac9a81bcbabbba6edf83f1885b749074e77500.jpg)*Helge Nelson*

 

### **Game Four**

 

Nelson had [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) on turn one, Dreger had [Mental Misstep](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mental+Misstep). Nelson tried [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage), Dreger had [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak). When he continued to not do anything on his turn, it became clear that Dreger's deck was giving him the full counter suite this time.

Next, he countered [Manabarbs](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Manabarbs) with [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze), then made a [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) and allowed [Chandra's Phoenix](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chandra%27s+Phoenix). The Phoenix swung in for 4 thanks to [Teetering Peaks](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Teetering+Peaks) and was joined on the following turn by [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide). Dreger really had to manage his resources, one of which was [Pyroclasm](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyroclasm) and killed both of Nelson's creatures. Another couple of spells were traded against counters which left Dreger with a fully active [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension), but no cards in hand, to his opponent's [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer). He drew his card and made a face of pleasant surprise. Down came [Batterskull](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Batterskull). Nelson found no way to handle the Skull and was competently battered.

Tobias Dreger 2 – 2 Helge Nelson

Dreger made a little adjustment to his deck, switching the third [Pyroclasm](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyroclasm) for a third [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil).

 

### **Game Five**

 

Nelson was happy to play first in the final game, but not too happy with his opening seven, happy enough, though, to keep. Dreger had the first play with [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain), Nelson had the very important [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage). Dreger cast a second [Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) and triggered his Ascension.

At the end of his turn, Nelson cast [Staggershock](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Staggershock), which Dreger stopped with [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze). Nelson untapped, played a fourth land and cast [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Koth+of+the+Hammer). On his turn, Dreger cast [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) on [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Koth%2Bof%2Bthe%2BHammer) and left mana up to be able to counter the planeswalker if needs be. Nelson chose to play [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Grim+Lavamancer) instead, leaving mana up for [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage), which had six counters already.

Dreger had [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) for the [Grim Lavamancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Grim%2BLavamancer), but apart from that all he had left was [Mental Misstep](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mental+Misstep) and [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze), and nothing with which to advance his [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) status. Meanwhile, Nelson was prepared to wait for his Shrine to reach lethality.

[Preordain](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Preordain) changed the plans, however, because a) it revved [Pyromancer Ascension](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pyromancer+Ascension) and b) found [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil). When Dreger cast it on [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage), it dealt 9 damage and he went to 6. But now Nelson actually had to fight to get rid of the rest. [Koth of the Hammer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Koth+of+the+Hammer) met [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze), [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide), however, went unimpeded directly into the red zone and revealed Dreger's next draw to be [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak). Nelson cast [Burst Lightning](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Burst+Lightning) to put Dreger at 2. [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) didn't really help.

**Tobias Dreger 2 – 3 Helge Nelson**

![](https://i.ormos.uk/73/29/7329cb2fe4242d77507434a2f994bacb515b8b228438c4a27d243bd918d04633.jpg)*Congratulations to Helge Nelson, the German National Champion 2011!*

## Top 5 Cards

German Nationals bore witness to the upheaval of certain well-established facts. Especially, the event surprised everybody with a new order to a Standard format that seemed to be so set in stone.

![Shrine of Burning Rage](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage)

### [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage)

This card destroyed the hopes of a number of U/B Control players. With typically just one [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) and one [Karn Liberated](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Karn+Liberated), people found themselves unable to deal with a threat which came down on turn two and won many a game virtually by itself. Also, the [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)-[Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) players, nowadays often eschewing [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring), had their fair share of difficulties.

![Auramancer](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Auramancer)

### [Auramancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Auramancer)

A typical value card, [Auramancer](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Auramancer) returned everything from [Divine Favor](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Divine+Favor) to [Pacifism](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Pacifism), from [Ice Cage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ice+Cage) to [Brink of Disaster](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brink+of+Disaster) in the *M12* drafts this weekend. One of the hallmarks of a good player was to know when to hold it back to return an enchantment later on, and when to play it early as a simple bear.

![Goblin Guide](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Goblin+Guide)

### [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide)

[Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) was the cornerstone of the red deck. The immediate impact it had every time it hit the battlefield on turn one was central to the red decks' success. On the other hand, whenever the early aggression petered out, a couple of extra lands boosted the opponents' chances to turn the game around. [Goblin Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Goblin+Guide) is a two-edged sword.

![Into the Roil](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Into+the+Roil)

### [Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil)

[Into the Roil](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Into+the+Roil) may look innocuous enough, but its versatility knew to impress. A number of [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)-[Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) players swore by the universal solution. For other decks it was the only line of defense against noncreature permanents. Timing it correctly was not always obvious, especially in the ubiquitous staring contest against a constantly growing [Shrine of Burning Rage](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Shrine+of+Burning+Rage). It was not easy to use, but essential to have.

![Squadron Hawk](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Squadron+Hawk)

### [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk)

[Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Squadron+Hawk) is a card of the weekend not because of its impact, but specifically because of the lack of impact. 44 out of 195 players had pinned their hopes on the [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk), and all of them were disappointed. No [Squadron Hawk](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Squadron%2BHawk)-[Sword of Feast and Famine](https://web.archive.org/web/20170721050316/http://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?&name=Sword%2Bof%2BFeast%2Band%2BFamine) made it to the Top 8. Compared to the results of previous Nationals, this was surprising to say the least. This weekend has severely ruffled its feathers.
